*Vincent Degenhart,Dewi Timman,Arjen P. de Vries,Faegheh Hasibi,Mohanna Hoveyda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了当前检索模型在处理复杂信息需求（如集合组合查询）时的表现，发现尽管神经检索器在QUEST基准上表现优于BM25，但在更严格的LIMIT+基准（强调约束满足而非语义捷径）上性能急剧下降，而传统词法检索反而表现更好；同时，所有方法随组合深度增加均出现性能下降，稀疏/词法方法更稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索范式是否真正满足集合组合查询（合取、析取、排除）的逻辑约束，还是仅依赖预训练知识中的‘语义捷径’，尚不明确。

Method: 开展可复现性研究，在QUEST及变体和新提出的LIMIT+基准上评测主流检索家族与推理导向方法（如ReasonIR、Search-R1）；LIMIT+被设计为依赖任意属性谓词与显式约束满足，弱化预训练知识影响。

Result: （i）QUEST上神经检索器Recall@100 > 0.41，显著优于BM25（0.20），但推理方法未普遍超越通用检索器；（ii）LIMIT+上最强QUEST方法性能骤降至<0.02，而BM25升至~0.96；（iii）随组合深度增加，所有方法性能持续下降，稀疏/词法方法更稳定，稠密方法崩溃明显。

Conclusion: 当前检索模型在依赖语义捷径的任务（QUEST）中表现良好，但在需严格逻辑约束满足的任务（LIMIT+）中严重失效，表明其缺乏真正的组合推理能力；词法方法在约束驱动场景下更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Complex information needs may involve set-compositional queries using conjunction, disjunction, and exclusion, yet it remains unclear whether current retrieval paradigms genuinely satisfy such constraints or exploit `semantic shortcuts'. We conduct a reproducibility study to benchmark major retrieval families and reasoning-targeted methods on QUEST and QUEST+Variants, and introduce LIMIT+, a controlled benchmark where relevance depends on arbitrary attribute predicates and constraint satisfaction, and less on pretrained knowledge. Our findings show that (i) on QUEST, the best neural retrievers achieve an effectiveness that is more than double what can be achieved with BM25 (Recall@100 ${>}$0.41 vs.\ 0.20), but reasoning-targeted methods like ReasonIR and Search-R1 do not outperform general-purpose retrievers uniformly; (ii) on LIMIT+, gains fail to transfer, where the strongest QUEST method collapses from Recall@100${\approx}$0.42 to below 0.02, while classic lexical retrieval gains to ${\sim}$0.96. Lastly, (iii) stratifying by compositional depth reveals a consistent degradation across all methods, where algebraic sparse and lexical methods show more stable performance while dense approaches collapse. We release code and LIMIT+ data generation scripts to support future reproducibility and controlled evaluation.

</details>


### [39] [TRACE: A Metrologically-Grounded Engineering Framework for Trustworthy Agentic AI Systems in Operationally Critical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03838)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE is a cross-domain framework for trustworthy agentic AI in critical domains, featuring a four-layer architecture, classical-ML vs. LLM-validator split, stateful orchestration, bounded human supervision, metrologically grounded trust metrics, and the Model-Parsimony principle quantified by Computational Parsimony Ratio (CPR).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To enable trustworthy agentic AI in operationally critical domains (e.g., clinical, industrial, judicial) where reliability, interpretability, and governance rigor are essential.

Method: Design and instantiation of TRACE: a four-layer reference architecture with explicit classical-ML/LLM-validator separation (L2a/L2b), stateful orchestration-and-escalation policy (L3), bounded human supervision (L4), metrologically grounded trust metrics aligned with GUM/VIM/ISO 17025, and the Model-Parsimony principle formalized via Computational Parsimony Ratio (CPR).

Result: Three domain-specific instantiations (clinical decision support, industrial multi-domain operations, judicial AI assistant) successfully transfer the same architecture and trust metrics across distinct governance contexts; CPR is established as a quantifiable, first-class design principle for trustworthy AI engineering.

Conclusion: TRACE provides a rigorous, cross-domain engineering foundation for trustworthy agentic AI, emphasizing deliberate LLM usage, metrological traceability, and computational parsimony—enabling safer, auditable, and governable AI in high-stakes applications.

Abstract: We introduce TRACE, a cross-domain engineering framework for trustworthy agentic AI in operationally critical domains. TRACE combines a four-layer reference architecture with an explicit classical-ML vs. LLM-validator split (L2a/L2b), a stateful orchestration-and-escalation policy (L3), and bounded human supervision (L4); a metrologically grounded trust-metric suite mapped to GUM/VIM/ISO 17025; and a Model-Parsimony principle quantified by the Computational Parsimony Ratio (CPR). Three instantiations--clinical decision support, industrial multi-domain operations, and a judicial AI assistant--transfer the samearchitecture and metrics across principally different governance contexts. The L2a/L2b separation makes the use of large language models a deliberate design decision rather than an architectural default, with parsimony quantified through CPR. TRACE introduces CPR as a first-class design principle in trustworthy-AI engineering.

</details>


### [40] [MCJudgeBench: A Benchmark for Constraint-Level Judge Evaluation in Multi-Constraint Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03858)
*Jaeyun Lee,Junyoung Koh,Zeynel Tok,Hunar Batra,Ronald Clark*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MCJudgeBench基准，用于在多约束指令遵循任务中评估大语言模型（LLM）作为评判器在约束级别上的可靠性，揭示了整体表现好不等于各约束类别判断一致可靠，尤其对'部分满足'和'不满足'等稀有标签；推理增强可提升正确性但未必提升稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评判器多依赖整体响应判断，缺乏对多约束指令中各独立约束满足情况的细粒度评估，难以识别其在不同约束类型上的可靠性差异与失败模式。

Method: 构建MCJudgeBench基准：包含指令、候选响应、显式约束列表、每约束黄金标签（yes/partial/no）及受控响应扰动；设计多种评估提示变体以测试评判器稳定性；采用正确性与不一致性（内在/程序性）双维度指标评测主流闭源与开源LLM评判器。

Result: 发现评判器可靠性具有多维性：高整体正确率不保证各标签类别（尤其partial/no）判断可靠；高正确性不必然对应低不一致性；引入推理可提升正确性，但对稳定性改善不具普适性。

Conclusion: 应推动在约束级别上评估LLM评判器，以深入分析其细粒度失败机制，并为构建更鲁棒、可解释的自动评估方法提供基础。

Abstract: Multi-constraint instruction following requires verifying whether a response satisfies multiple individual requirements, yet LLM judges are often assessed only through overall-response judgments. We introduce MCJudgeBench, a benchmark for constraint-level judge evaluation in multi-constraint instruction following. Each instance includes an instruction, a candidate response, an explicit constraint list, per-constraint gold labels in {yes, partial, no}, and controlled response-side perturbations. The evaluation protocol further includes evaluation prompt variants to test judge stability. We evaluate proprietary and open-source LLM judges using both correctness and inconsistency metrics, distinguishing intrinsic inconsistency under stochastic decoding from procedural inconsistency under prompt and response perturbations. Our results show that judge reliability has multiple dimensions: strong overall performance does not guarantee equally reliable detection across label categories, especially for rarer partial and no cases. Judges with higher correctness do not always have lower inconsistency. Evaluation with reasoning improves correctness but does not uniformly improve stability. These findings motivate evaluating LLM judges at the constraint level to study these failure modes.

</details>


### [41] [CC-OCR V2: Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Literacy in Real-world Document Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03903)
*Zhipeng Xu,Junhao Ji,Zulong Chen,Zhenghao Liu,Qing Liu,Chunyi Peng,Zubao Qin,Ze Xu,Jianqiang Wan,Jun Tang,Zhibo Yang,Shuai Bai,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向真实世界文档处理的综合性OCR基准CC-OCR V2，涵盖5个OCR核心任务、7093个高难度样本，实验表明当前大语言多模态模型在实际应用中仍存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR基准任务范围与实际应用脱节，且假设采集条件均一，无法反映真实场景复杂性，因此需要构建更贴近现实的评估基准。

Method: 构建了CC-OCR V2基准，覆盖文本识别、文档解析、文档定位、关键信息抽取和文档问答五大OCR核心任务，包含大量困难和边缘案例，并对14种先进大 multimodal 模型进行系统评测。

Result: 实验显示当前最先进LMMs在各类真实场景下性能显著下降，暴露出与实际应用需求之间的巨大差距。

Conclusion: 现有LMMs虽在传统OCR基准上表现良好，但在真实企业级文档处理任务中仍远未达标，亟需更鲁棒、更实用的模型设计与评估范式。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently shown strong performance on Optical Character Recognition (OCR) tasks, demonstrating their promising capability in document literacy. However, their effectiveness in real-world applications remains underexplored, as existing benchmarks adopt task scopes misaligned with practical applications and assume homogeneous acquisition conditions. To address this gap, we introduce CC-OCR V2, a comprehensive and challenging OCR benchmark tailored to real-world document processing. CC-OCR V2 focuses on practical enterprise document processing tasks and incorporates hard and corner cases that are critical yet underrepresented in prior benchmarks, covering 5 major OCR-centric tracks: text recognition, document parsing, document grounding, key information extraction, and document question answering, comprising 7,093 high-difficulty samples. Extensive experiments on 14 advanced LMMs reveal that current models fall short of real-world application requirements. Even state-of-the-art LMMs exhibit substantial performance degradation across diverse tasks and scenarios. These findings reveal a significant gap between performance on current benchmarks and effectiveness in real-world applications. We release the full dataset and evaluation toolkit at https://github.com/eioss/CC-OCR-V2.

</details>


### [42] [Steer Like the LLM: Activation Steering that Mimics Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03907)
*Geert Heyman,Frederik Vandeputte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR)模型，将提示引导（prompt steering）建模为基于激活的引导，并通过从激活中估计token级引导系数来模仿提示引导行为，在多个基准上优于现有激活引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活干预引导方法在性能上常不如提示引导，且未忠实反映提示引导中对部分token强干预、其余几乎不干预的机制。

Method: 提出PSR模型，从模型激活中估计token特定的引导系数，并训练其模仿提示引导行为；将提示引导形式化为一种激活引导问题。

Result: 在三个引导基准和多个大语言模型上的实验表明，PSR显著优于现有激活引导方法，尤其在高连贯性生成任务中；在AxBench和角色引导任务上也媲美甚至优于提示引导。

Conclusion: 提示引导可被有效建模为激活引导，PSR通过可解释、轻量的激活映射实现高性能引导，弥合了激活引导与提示引导之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Large language models can be steered at inference time through prompting or activation interventions, but activation steering methods often underperform compared to prompt-based approaches. We propose a framework that formulates prompt steering as a form of activation steering and investigates whether distilling successful prompt steering behavior into simpler, interpretable models can close this gap. Our analysis reveals that popular activation steering methods are not faithful to the mechanics of prompt steering, which applies strong interventions on some tokens while barely affecting others. Based on these insights, we introduce Prompt Steering Replacement (PSR) models that estimate token-specific steering coefficients from the activations themselves and are trained to imitate prompt-based interventions. Experiments on three steering benchmarks across multiple language models show that PSR models outperform existing activation steering methods, especially when controlling for high-coherence completions, and also compare favorably to prompting on AxBench and persona steering.

</details>


### [43] [Atomic Fact-Checking Increases Clinician Trust in Large Language Model Recommendations for Oncology Decision Support: A Randomized Controlled Trial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03916)
*Lisa C. Adams,Linus Marx,Erik Thiele Orberg,Keno Bressem,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Denise Bernhardt,Markus Graf,Marcus R. Makowski,Stephanie E. Combs,Florian Matthes,Jan C. Peeken*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过随机对照试验发现，原子事实核查（将AI治疗建议分解为与指南文档链接的可单独验证声明）显著提高了临床医生对AI建议的信任度，效果远超传统可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究原子事实核查是否比传统的可解释性方法更能提升临床医生对AI治疗建议的信任。

Method: 开展一项包含356名临床医生、生成7476条信任评分的随机对照试验，比较原子事实核查与传统透明机制对信任度的影响。

Result: 原子事实核查使信任医生比例从26.9%升至66.5%，效应量Cohen's d = 0.94；传统方法效应量仅为d = 0.25–0.50。

Conclusion: 在高风险临床决策中，将AI建议分解为可验证、指南支撑的原子化声明，能显著提升临床医生信任，优于传统可解释性方法。

Abstract: Question: Does atomic fact-checking, which decomposes AI treatment recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guideline documents, increase clinician trust compared to traditional explainability approaches?
  Findings: In this randomized trial of 356 clinicians generating 7,476 trust ratings, atomic fact-checking produced a large effect on trust (Cohen's d = 0.94), increasing the proportion of clinicians expressing trust from 26.9% to 66.5%. Traditional transparency mechanisms showed a dose-response gradient of improvement over baseline (d = 0.25 to 0.50).
  Meaning: Decomposing AI recommendations into individually verifiable claims linked to source guidelines produces substantially higher clinician trust than traditional explainability approaches in high-stakes clinical decisions.

</details>


### [44] [The Counterexample Game: Iterated Conceptual Analysis and Repair in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03936)
*Daniel Drucker,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型（LM）是否能通过迭代的‘分析-修复’链（即生成反例→修正定义→再生成反例）执行哲学中的概念分析任务；结果发现：LM生成的反例多被人类专家判定为无效，但LM裁判接受率是人类的两倍；人与LM在有效性判断上具中等一致性；持续迭代导致定义冗长化但未提升准确性；部分概念本身难以稳定定义；整体表明LM可参与哲学推理，但该循环易陷入收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型能否模拟哲学中核心的方法论——通过提出定义、构造反例、反复修正来实现概念分析。

Method: 设计迭代的‘反例生成–定义修复’链：由一个LM实例生成反例，另一个LM实例据此修复定义，循环进行；在20个哲学概念上开展数千次循环，并由人类专家和LM裁判分别评估反例有效性及定义质量。

Result: 1）多数LM生成的反例被人类专家判为无效，但LM裁判接受率约为人类的两倍；2）人类内部及人-LM之间对单个反例的有效性判断呈中等一致性；3）延长迭代轮次导致定义显著变长，但准确率未提升；4）部分概念在多轮迭代后仍无法获得稳定定义。

Conclusion: 语言模型虽能形式化参与哲学概念分析，但‘反例–修复’循环存在快速收益递减现象，难以支撑高质量、可持续的高阶迭代哲学推理；该任务可作为评测LM高阶推理能力的有力基准。

Abstract: Conceptual analysis -- proposing definitions and refining them through counterexamples -- is central to philosophical methodology. We study whether language models can perform this task through iterated analysis and repair chains: one model instance generates counterexamples to a proposed definition, another repairs the definition, and the process repeats. Across 20 concepts and thousands of counterexample-repair cycles, we find that, although many LM-generated counterexamples are judged invalid by both expert humans and an LM judge, the LM judge accepts roughly twice as many as humans do. Nonetheless, per-item validity judgments are moderately consistent across humans and between humans and the LM. We further find that extended iteration produces increasingly verbose definitions without improving accuracy. We also see that some concepts resist stable definitions in general. These findings suggest that while LMs can engage in philosophical reasoning, the counterexample-repair loop hits diminishing returns quickly and could be a fruitful test case for evaluating whether LMs can sustain high-level iterated philosophical reasoning.

</details>


### [45] [Feature-Augmented Transformers for Robust AI-Text Detection Across Domains and Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03969)
*Mohamed Mady,Johannes Reschke,Björn Schuller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI生成文本检测器在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，提出基于DeBERTa-v3和注意力机制特征融合的方法，在跨数据集迁移任务中显著提升性能，并通过固定阈值协议更真实地评估实际鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生成文本广泛存在且来源多样，导致检测器面临严重的分布偏移问题，需提升其在不同域和生成器下的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用HC3 PLUS训练基于Transformer的检测器，采用固定决策阈值（基于验证集平衡准确率最大化），并引入注意力机制进行语言学特征融合增强；在M4和AI-Text-Detection-Pile等多源数据上评估跨域泛化能力。

Result: DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn模型在M4基准上达到85.9%平衡准确率，显著优于零样本基线（+7.22点）；可读性和词汇特征对鲁棒性贡献最大；多随机种子实验显示高稳定性。

Conclusion: 特征增强与现代DeBERTa架构能显著超越早期BERT/RoBERTa模型；固定阈值协议更贴近实际部署场景，是评估检测器鲁棒性的更优范式。

Abstract: AI-generated text is nowadays produced at scale across domains and heterogeneous generation pipelines, making robustness to distribution shift a central requirement for supervised binary detectors. We train transformer-based detectors on HC3 PLUS and calibrate a single decision threshold by maximising balanced accuracy on held-out validation; this threshold is then kept fixed for all downstream test distributions, revealing domain- and generator-dependent error asymmetries under shift. We evaluate in-domain on HC3 PLUS, under cross-dataset transfer to the multi-domain, multi-generator M4 benchmark, and on the external AI-Text-Detection-Pile. Although base models achieve near-ceiling in-domain performance (up to 99.5% balanced accuracy), performance under shift is brittle and strongly model-dependent. Feature augmentation via attention-based linguistic feature fusion improves transfer, with our best model (DeBERTa-v3-base+FeatAttn) achieving 85.9% balanced accuracy on M4. Multi-seed experiments confirm high stability. Under the same fixed-threshold protocol, our model outperforms strong zero-shot baselines by up to +7.22 points. Category-level ablations further show that readability and vocabulary features contribute most to robustness under shift. Overall, these results demonstrate that feature augmentation and a modern DeBERTa backbone significantly outperform earlier BERT/RoBERTa models, while the fixed-threshold protocol provides a more realistic and informative assessment of practical detector robustness.

</details>


### [46] [Logical Consistency as a Bridge: Improving LLM Hallucination Detection via Label Constraint Modeling between Responses and Self-Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03971)
*Hao Mi,Qiang Sheng,Shaofei Wang,Beizhe Hu,Yifan Sun,Zhengjia Wang,Hengqi Zeng,Yang Li,Danding Wang,Juan Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LaaB框架，通过将符号化判断映射回神经特征空间，利用响应与元判断标签间的逻辑关系（相同或相反）实现神经与符号信号的对齐与互学习，从而提升大语言模型幻觉检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法仅关注神经不确定性或符号化推理中的单一层面，未能利用二者内在耦合关系进行联合建模。

Method: 提出LaaB（Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge）框架，引入‘元判断’过程将符号标签映射回特征空间，并基于响应与元判断标签的逻辑一致性（相同/相反）进行双视角信号对齐和互学习。

Result: 在4个公开数据集、4种大语言模型及8种基线方法上的大量实验表明，LaaB显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 神经表征与符号判断可通过逻辑一致性桥接并协同优化，LaaB为幻觉检测提供了更全面、鲁棒的双视角建模范式。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to factual hallucinations, risking their reliability in real-world applications. Existing hallucination detectors mainly extract micro-level intrinsic patterns for uncertainty quantification or elicit macro-level self-judgments through verbalized prompts. However, these methods address only a single facet of the hallucination, focusing either on implicit neural uncertainty or explicit symbolic reasoning, thereby treating these inherently coupled behaviors in isolation and failing to exploit their interdependence for a holistic view. In this paper, we propose LaaB (Logical Consistency-as-a-Bridge), a framework that bridges neural features and symbolic judgments for hallucination detection. LaaB introduces a "meta-judgment" process to map symbolic labels back into the feature space. By leveraging the inherent logical bridge where response and meta-judgment labels are either the same or opposite based on the self-judgment's semantics, LaaB aligns and integrates dual-view signals via mutual learning and enhances the hallucination detection. Extensive experiments on 4 public datasets, across 4 LLMs, against 8 baselines demonstrate the superiority of LaaB.

</details>


### [47] [EQUITRIAGE: A Fairness Audit of Gender Bias in LLM-Based Emergency Department Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03998)
*Richard J. Young,Alice M. Matthews*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对五种大语言模型在急诊分诊（ESI）任务中的性别公平性进行了系统审计，发现所有模型均存在显著的性别相关偏差（翻转率9.9%-43.8%），部分模型表现出女性被系统性低分诊倾向；研究揭示了校准性与反事实不变性可分离、干预效果因模型而异等关键结论，并强调临床部署前须开展模型专属的反事实公平性审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中已知人类分诊存在性别差异，而医院正试点将大语言模型（LLM）用于分诊决策支持，亟需评估这些模型是否会复现或缓解既有偏见。

Method: 提出EQUITRIAGE框架，对5个主流LLM（Gemini-3-Flash、Nemotron-3-Super、DeepSeek-V3.1、Mistral-Small-3.2、GPT-4.1-Nano）在MIMIC-IV-ED数据集的18,714个急诊病例（共374,275次评估）上进行公平性审计；采用四种提示策略，含9,346组性别交换反事实对照；评估指标包括翻转率、性别方向偏差、校准差距、反事实不变性及干预（如去标识化、思维链）效果。

Result: 所有模型翻转率均超5%阈值（9.9%–43.8%）；DeepSeek和Gemini呈现女性低估倾向（F/M比分别为2.15:1和1.34:1）；Demographic blinding大幅降低Gemini翻转率（至0.5%），但DeepSeek仍有残余偏差（F/M 1.25:1），提示年龄为残余通道；思维链提示反而降低全部模型准确性；双模型消融显示相同偏差现象由不同机制驱动（Gemini依赖姓名+性别联合信号，DeepSeek仅靠性别标记）。

Conclusion: 群体间平等、反事实不变性与性别校准是三个相互独立的公平性维度；公平干预效果高度依赖具体模型；不能以单一指标或通用方法评估LLM分诊公平性；必须对每个拟部署模型开展定制化反事实审计。

Abstract: Emergency department triage assigns patients an acuity score that determines treatment priority, and clinical evidence documents persistent gender disparities in human acuity assessment. As hospitals pilot large language models (LLMs) as triage decision support, a critical question is whether these models reproduce or mitigate known biases. We present EQUITRIAGE, a fairness audit of LLM-based ESI assignment evaluating five models (Gemini-3-Flash, Nemotron-3-Super, DeepSeek-V3.1, Mistral-Small-3.2, GPT-4.1-Nano) across 374,275 evaluations on 18,714 MIMIC-IV-ED vignettes under four prompt strategies. Of 9,368 originals, 9,346 are paired with a gender-swapped counterfactual. All five models produced flip rates above a pre-registered 5% threshold (9.9% to 43.8%). Two showed directional female undertriage (DeepSeek F/M 2.15:1, Gemini 1.34:1); two were near-parity; one had high sensitivity with weak male-direction asymmetry. DeepSeek's directional bias coexisted with a low outcome-linked calibration gap (0.013 against MIMIC-IV admission), a Chouldechova-style dissociation between within-group calibration and between-pair counterfactual invariance. Demographic blinding reduced Gemini's flip rate to 0.5%; an age-preserving blind variant left DeepSeek with residual F/M 1.25, implicating age as a residual channel. Chain-of-thought prompting degraded accuracy for all five models. A two-model ablation reveals opposite underlying mechanisms for the same directional phenotype: in Gemini the signal is emergent in the combined name+gender swap, while in DeepSeek the gender token alone carries it. EQUITRIAGE shows that group parity, counterfactual invariance, and gender calibration are distinct fairness properties, that intervention effectiveness is model-dependent, and that per-model counterfactual auditing should precede clinical deployment.

</details>

*Yilun Zhao,Jinbiao Wei,Tingyu Song,Siyue Zhang,Chen Zhao,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BRIGHT-Pro基准测试和RTriever-Synth合成语料库，以改进推理密集型检索的评估与训练，提升检索器在多方面证据获取与智能体搜索场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理密集型检索的评估基准（如BRIGHT）覆盖范围窄、仅孤立评估检索器；合成训练数据多关注单段相关性，忽视证据组合构建，难以支撑智能体式迭代检索与综合需求。

Method: 构建专家标注的BRIGHT-Pro基准（支持多方面黄金证据与静态/智能体搜索协议评估），并设计面向方面的合成语料RTriever-Synth（含互补正样本与条件难负样本），在此基础上对Qwen3-Embedding-4B的RTriever-4B模型进行LoRA微调。

Result: 实验表明：面向方面的评估与智能体式协议能揭示传统指标掩盖的检索行为缺陷；RTriever-4B在各类检索器中显著优于其基础模型。

Conclusion: 面向方面建模与智能体协同评估是提升推理密集型检索能力的关键路径，BRIGHT-Pro与RTriever-Synth为该方向提供了更贴近实际需求的评估与训练范式。

Abstract: Reasoning-intensive retrieval aims to surface evidence that supports downstream reasoning rather than merely matching topical similarity. This capability is increasingly important for agentic search systems, where retrievers must provide complementary evidence across iterative search and synthesis. However, existing work remains limited on both evaluation and training: benchmarks such as BRIGHT provide narrow gold sets and evaluate retrievers in isolation, while synthetic training corpora often optimize single-passage relevance rather than evidence portfolio construction. We introduce BRIGHT-Pro, an expert-annotated benchmark that expands each query with multi-aspect gold evidence and evaluates retrievers under both static and agentic search protocols. We further construct RTriever-Synth, an aspect-decomposed synthetic corpus that generates complementary positives and positive-conditioned hard negatives, and use it to LoRA fine-tune RTriever-4B from Qwen3-Embedding-4B. Experiments across lexical, general-purpose, and reasoning-intensive retrievers show that aspect-aware and agentic evaluation expose behaviors hidden by standard metrics, while RTriever-4B substantially improves over its base model.

</details>


### [49] [Safety and accuracy follow different scaling laws in clinical large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04039)
*Sebastian Wind,Tri-Thien Nguyen,Jeta Sopa,Mahshad Lotfinia,Sebastian Bickelhaup,Michael Uder,Harald Köstler,Gerhard Wellein,Sven Nebelung,Daniel Truhn,Andreas Maier,Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SaFE-Scale框架，用于系统评估临床大语言模型（LLM）在不同扩展维度（如模型规模、证据质量、检索策略等）下的安全性变化，并构建了放射学安全评估基准RadSaFE-200；实验发现高质量证据显著提升安全性，而单纯增加模型规模或推理计算量效果有限，安全性主要取决于部署设计而非被动缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床LLM中，少数高置信度、高风险或违背医学证据的错误可能比平均准确率更重要，但现有研究常隐含‘更高准确率即更安全’的不完整假设，亟需专门衡量安全性的框架。

Method: 提出SaFE-Scale安全性评估框架，并构建RadSaFE-200放射科安全评估基准（含200道多选题，标注清洁/冲突证据、高风险错误、不安全答案及证据矛盾）；在34个本地部署LLM上对比六种部署条件（闭卷提示、清洁证据、冲突证据、标准RAG、智能体RAG、最大上下文提示）的安全性表现。

Result: 清洁证据使平均准确率从73.5%升至94.1%，高风险错误从12.0%降至2.6%，证据矛盾从12.7%降至2.3%，危险性过度自信从8.0%降至1.6%；而标准RAG与智能体RAG未能复现该安全收益，最大上下文提示和额外推理算力提升有限；最差情况分析显示严重错误集中在少数题目。

Conclusion: 临床LLM的安全性并非模型缩放的自然结果，而是由证据质量、检索设计、上下文构建及整体失效行为共同决定的部署属性，需主动设计而非被动依赖规模增长。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs are often scaled by increasing model size, context length, retrieval complexity, or inference-time compute, with the implicit expectation that higher accuracy implies safer behavior. This assumption is incomplete in medicine, where a few confident, high-risk, or evidence-contradicting errors can matter more than average benchmark performance. We introduce SaFE-Scale, a framework for measuring how clinical LLM safety changes across model scale, evidence quality, retrieval strategy, context exposure, and inference-time compute. To instantiate this framework, we introduce RadSaFE-200, a Radiology Safety-Focused Evaluation benchmark of 200 multiple-choice questions with clinician-defined clean evidence, conflict evidence, and option-level labels for high-risk error, unsafe answer, and evidence contradiction. We evaluated 34 locally deployed LLMs across six deployment conditions: closed-book prompting (zero-shot), clean evidence, conflict evidence, standard RAG, agentic RAG, and max-context prompting. Clean evidence produced the strongest improvement, increasing mean accuracy from 73.5% to 94.1%, while reducing high-risk error from 12.0% to 2.6%, contradiction from 12.7% to 2.3%, and dangerous overconfidence from 8.0% to 1.6%. Standard RAG and agentic RAG did not reproduce this safety profile: agentic RAG improved accuracy over standard RAG and reduced contradiction, but high-risk error and dangerous overconfidence remained elevated. Max-context prompting increased latency without closing the safety gap, and additional inference-time compute produced only limited gains. Worst-case analysis showed that clinically consequential errors concentrated in a small subset of questions. Clinical LLM safety is therefore not a passive consequence of scaling, but a deployment property shaped by evidence quality, retrieval design, context construction, and collective failure behavior.

</details>

### [50] [Memorization In Stable Diffusion Is Unexpectedly Driven by CLIP Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02908)
*Bumjun Kim,Albert No*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Stable Diffusion中CLIP嵌入对文本到图像生成中记忆化现象的非预期影响，发现<pad>嵌入因结构上重复<eot>嵌入而显著加剧模型对<eot>嵌入的过度依赖，从而驱动记忆化；据此提出两种无需预检测、即插即用的推理时缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解文本嵌入如何影响文生图扩散模型的记忆化行为，对提升模型可解释性与安全性至关重要。

Method: 通过分析Stable Diffusion中CLIP嵌入各token（<startoftext>, <prompt>, <endoftext>, <pad>）的贡献，识别出<pad>嵌入因结构复制<eot>嵌入而放大其影响；进而提出两种推理时干预方法：①将tokenizer默认<pad>替换为!并掩码<v^eot>；②部分掩码<v^pad>。

Result: 两种策略均能有效抑制记忆化，且不损害生成质量，且无需事先检测记忆化样本，易于部署。

Conclusion: 记忆化主要源于CLIP训练中仅优化<eot>嵌入，而<pad>嵌入无意中复制其结构，导致模型过依赖<eot>；所提轻量级推理干预方案为安全可控的文生图生成提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Understanding how textual embeddings contribute to memorization in text-to-image diffusion models is crucial for both interpretability and safety. This paper investigates an unexpected behavior of CLIP embeddings in Stable Diffusion, revealing that the model disproportionately relies on specific embeddings. We categorize input tokens as <startoftext>, <prompt>, <endoftext> and <pad> with corresponding embeddings $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{sot}}, \mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pr}}, \mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{eot}}, \mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pad}}$. We discover that $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pr}}$ contribute minimally to generation in memorized cases. In contrast, $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pad}}$ strongly affect memorization due to their structural duplication of $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{eot}}$, the only embedding explicitly optimized during CLIP training. This duplication unintentionally amplifies the influence of $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{eot}}$, causing the model to over-rely on it, thereby driving memorization. Based on these observations, we propose two simple yet effective inference-time mitigation strategies: (1) Replacing the tokenizer's default <pad> from <eot> to the ! token before embedding, and masking the $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{eot}}$; (2) Partial masking of $\mathbf{v}^{\mathbf{pad}}$. Both suppress memorization without degrading quality, and are readily deployable without prior detection.

</details>


### [51] [Reasoning-Guided Grounding: Elevating Video Anomaly Detection through Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02912)
*Sakshi Agarwal,Aishik Konwer,Ankit Parag Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VANGUARD是一个统一视频异常检测中分类、空间定位与思维链推理的VLM框架，通过三阶段课程学习和教师-学生标注流水线，在UCF-Crime上实现高精度检测并提供可解释性与定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频异常检测缺乏可解释性和精确空间定位；现有视觉语言模型虽具场景理解能力，但空间定位不可靠、易幻觉。

Method: 提出VANGUARD框架，采用三阶段课程学习：（1）冻结骨干特征的分类器预热，（2）LoRA适配的空间定位，（3）思维链生成；结合Qwen3-VL-4B生成结构化子片段推理轨迹（基于UCA数据集人工标注），并用GroundingDINO提供边界框监督。

Result: 在UCF-Crime上达94% ROC-AUC与84% F1，同时输出可解释思维链和异常物体空间定位；消融实验证明分阶段训练更优，结构化推理起隐式正则作用；零样本迁移到XD-Violence和ShanghaiTech展现跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: VANGUARD首次在单一VLM中协同实现视频异常检测的判别性、定位性与可解释性，解决了传统方法与现有VLM在VAD任务中的关键局限。

Abstract: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) has traditionally been framed as binary classification or outlier detection, providing neither interpretable reasoning nor precise spatial localization of anomalous events. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer rich scene understanding, they struggle with reliable spatial grounding - often producing hallucinated or geometrically invalid bounding boxes when asked to localize objects. We propose VANGUARD (Video Anomaly Understanding through Reasoning and Grounding), a framework that unifies anomaly classification, spatial grounding, and chain-of-thought reasoning within a single VLM. VANGUARD introduces a three-stage curriculum that progressively layers training objectives: (1) classifier warmup on frozen backbone features, (2) LoRA-adapted spatial grounding, and (3) chain-of-thought generation. To overcome the sparse annotation typical of VAD benchmarks, we employ a teacher-student annotation pipeline in which a VLM (Qwen3-VL-4B) generates structured per-subclip reasoning trajectories based on manual annotations available from the UCA Dataset. Further, GroundingDINO provides bounding box supervision. On UCF-Crime, VANGUARD achieves 94% ROC-AUC with 84% F1 while simultaneously producing interpretable chain-of-thought explanations and spatial grounding of anomalous objects - capabilities absent from prior VAD methods. Ablations confirm that staged training outperforms monolithic optimization, and that structured reasoning acts as an implicit regularizer yielding more balanced predictions than classification-only fine-tuning. Zero-shot transfer to XD-Violence and ShanghaiTech demonstrates cross-domain generalization without target-domain adaptation.

</details>


### [52] [Approaching human parity in the quality of automated organoid image segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03053)
*Chase Cartwright,Gongbo Guo,Sai Teja Pusuluri,Christopher N. Mayhew,Mark Hester,Horacio E. Castillo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合通用基础模型SAM与领域专用工具的复合方法，用于自动、准确地测量iPSC来源类器官球体的大小和形状，其性能接近人工标注者间变异水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类器官发育过程动态复杂，需先进成像与分析工具来准确监测其生长轨迹和疾病进程；现有自动分割工具在多变实验条件下精度不足。

Method: 提出一种复合方法：将通用基础模型Segment Anything Model（SAM）与已有领域专用工具相结合，用于iPSC来源类器官球体图像的自动分割，并与多种现有工具及人工分割结果对比评估。

Result: 该复合方法在绝大多数测试图像上实现了稳定且高精度的分割效果，仅在极少数最具挑战性图像上表现不佳；其精度在一项指标上达到人工标注者间变异水平，在其他指标上也非常接近。

Conclusion: 该复合方法显著提升了类器官图像自动分析的鲁棒性与准确性，为类器官研究提供了可靠、可扩展的计算工具。

Abstract: Organoids are complex, three dimensional, self-organizing cell cultures which manifest organ-like features and represent a powerful platform for studying human disease and developing treatment options. Organoid development is characterized by dynamic morphological and cellular organization, which mimic some aspects of organ development. To study these rapid changes over the course of organoid development, advanced imaging and analytical tools are critical to accurately monitor the trajectory of organoid growth and investigate disease processes.
  In this work, we focus on computer vision and machine learning techniques to automatically measure the size and shape of developing spheroids derived from pluripotent stem cells (iPSCs), which are typically the starting material for generating organoid cultures. To facilitate this task, we introduce a composite method that combines the Segment Anything Model (SAM), a general-purpose foundation model, with an existing domain-specific tool. This composite method is evaluated together with several existing tools by testing them on organoid image data and comparing with the results of manual image segmentation. We find that no single existing tool is able to segment the test images with sufficient accuracy across all test conditions, but the newly introduced composite method produces consistent and accurate results for all but a very small fraction of the most challenging images. Finally, we compare the accuracy of this method to the variability between manual segmentations by independent annotators (inter-observer variability) and find that by one measure it performs at the level of inter-observer variability and by others it performs very close to it.

</details>


### [53] [Learning to Segment using Summary Statistics and Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03059)
*Omkar Kulkarni,Edward Raff,Tim Oates*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅依赖图像摘要统计（如区域面积）和少量弱监督像素信息来训练医学图像分割模型的新方法，通过结合图像重建、统计匹配和弱监督重叠的损失函数，在超声和CT数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减轻医学专家手动分割图像的负担，利用其通常保留的摘要统计信息（如区域面积）来训练分割模型。

Method: 设计了一种新颖的损失函数，融合图像重建质量、与摘要统计的匹配度以及预测前景与弱监督信号（少量像素）之间的重叠度。

Result: 实验证明，仅用统计信息效果有限，但加入少量弱监督像素后性能显著提升，在乳腺超声和肾脏肿瘤CT数据上均验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 摘要统计结合少量弱监督信号可有效支持医学图像分割模型训练，为减少人工标注负担提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Medical experts often manually segment images to obtain diagnostic statistics and discard the resulting annotations. We aim to train segmentation models to alleviate this burden, but constrained to the retained summary statistics (e.g., the area of the annotated region). Empirical results suggest that statistics alone are insufficient for this task, but adding weak information in the form of a few pixels within the area of interest significantly improves performance. We use a novel loss function that combines terms for image reconstruction quality, matching to summary statistics, and overlap between the predicted foreground and the weak supervisory signal. Experiments on standard image, ultrasound (breast cancer), and Computed Tomography (CT) scan (kidney tumors) data demonstrate the utility and potential of the approach.

</details>


### [54] [One Sequence to Segment Them All: Efficient Data Augmentation for CT and MRI Cross-Domain 3D Spine Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03098)
*Nathan Molinier,Hendrik Möller,Thomas Dagonneau,Anna Curto-Vilalta,Robert Graf,Matan Atad,Daniel Rueckert,Jan S. Kirschke,Julien Cohen-Adad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了针对跨模态迁移的数据增强技术，显著提升了脊柱分割模型在未见影像序列/模态（如CT与MRI间）上的泛化能力，同时保持原有域内性能，并通过GPU优化降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习医学图像分割受限于高质量标注数据稀缺及跨成像协议泛化能力不足，尤其在MRI和CT中，模型通常仅在单一序列上训练，难以适应未见序列或对比度；现有数据增强对跨模态泛化的定量影响尚未被系统研究。

Method: 设计并评估一组面向跨模态迁移的数据增强技术；在单模态/单序列数据集上训练三个脊柱分割模型，并在七个分布外数据集（涵盖CT和MRI）上测试；采用GPU优化实现高效增强。

Result: 在未见域上平均Dice系数提升155%，域内精度几乎不变（平均下降仅0.008%），实现了CT与MRI间的有效迁移；GPU优化使训练效率提升约10%。

Conclusion: 所提数据增强策略能显著增强模型在异构临床影像场景下的鲁棒性，且不牺牲训练速度，已开源并支持nnUNet和MONAI等主流框架。

Abstract: Deep learning-based medical image segmentation is increasingly used to support clinical diagnosis and develop new treatment strategies. However, model performance remains limited by the scarcity of high-quality annotated data and insufficient generalization across imaging protocols. This limitation is particularly evident in MRI and CT, where models are typically trained on a single acquisition sequence and exhibit reduced robustness when applied to unseen sequences or contrasts. Although data augmentation is widely used to improve general robustness on medical images, its impact on cross-modality generalization has not been quantitatively explored. In this work, we study a targeted set of data augmentation techniques designed to improve cross-modality transfer. We train three spine segmentation models, each on a single-modality/sequence dataset, and evaluate them across seven out-of-distribution datasets (spanning CT and MRI), reflecting a realistic single-sequence training and multi-sequence/contrast/modality deployment scenario. Our results demonstrate substantial performance gains on unseen domains (average Dice gain of 155 %) while preserving in-domain accuracy (average Dice decrease of 0.008 %), including effective transfer between CT and MRI. To mitigate the computational cost typically associated with strong data augmentation, we implement GPU-optimized augmentations that maintain, and even improve, training efficiency by approximately 10 %. We release our approach as an open-source toolbox, enabling seamless integration into commonly used frameworks such as nnUNet and MONAI. These augmentations significantly enhance robustness to heterogeneous clinical imaging scenarios without compromising training speed.

</details>


### [55] [NucEval: A Robust Evaluation Framework for Nuclear Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03144)
*Amirreza Mahbod,Ramona Woitek,Jeanne Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NucEval的统一评估框架，用于解决核实例分割中模糊区域处理、分数归一化、重叠实例和边界不确定性四个关键评估问题，并在多个数据集和模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核实例分割评估流程中存在四个被忽视的关键问题：模糊区域处理、分数归一化、重叠实例和边界不确定性，亟需系统性解决方案。

Method: 提出NucEval统一评估框架，整合针对上述四类问题的改进策略，并在NuInsSeg及两个外部数据集上，结合三种CNN/ViT模型进行验证。

Result: NucEval显著提升了核实例分割评估的鲁棒性与可靠性，代码、指南和示例已开源。

Conclusion: 标准化、鲁棒的评估流程对推动计算病理学中核实例分割研究至关重要，NucEval为该领域提供了可复现、可推广的评估基准。

Abstract: In computational pathology, nuclear instance segmentation is a fundamental task with many downstream clinical applications. With the advent of deep learning, many approaches, including convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and vision transformers (ViTs), have been proposed for this task, along with both machine learning-based and non-machine learning-based pre- and post-processing techniques to further boost performance. However, one fundamental aspect that has received less attention is the evaluation pipeline. In this study, we identify four key issues associated with nuclear instance segmentation evaluation and propose corresponding solutions. Our proposed modifications, namely handling vague regions, score normalization, overlapping instances, and border uncertainty, are integrated into a unified framework called NucEval, which enables robust evaluation of nuclear instance segmentation. We evaluate this pipeline using the NuInsSeg dataset, which provides unique characteristics that make it particularly suitable for this study, as well as two additional external datasets, with three CNN- and ViT-based nuclear instance segmentation models, to demonstrate the impact of these modifications on instance segmentation metrics. The code, along with complete guidelines and illustrative examples, is publicly available at: https://github.com/masih4/nuc_eval.

</details>


### [56] [Boundary-Aware Uncertainty Quantification for Wildfire Spread Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03148)
*Jonas V. Funk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向野火边界的不确定性量化评估框架FCER，用于更实际地评估深度学习模型在野火蔓延预测中的不确定性；并在WildfireSpreadTS数据集上验证了蒸馏后的单次前向学生模型在边界相关区域具有与集成模型相当的校准性能和互补的不确定性排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在野火蔓延预测中缺乏原则性的不确定性量化（UQ），且全局评估指标难以反映边界敏感场景下的实际性能需求。

Method: 提出Fire-Centered Evaluation Region（FCER）空间条件化评估框架，用于聚焦关键火区的不确定性量化分析；在WildfireSpreadTS数据集上对比集成模型与知识蒸馏所得单次前向学生模型的UQ性能。

Result: 学生模型在FCER定义的关键火区中展现出与集成模型相当的校准性，并在边界相关区域提供互补的不确定性排序。

Conclusion: FCER为野火预测UQ提供了更具操作意义的评估范式；轻量化的蒸馏模型可在保持UQ质量的同时提升推理效率，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Reliable wildfire spread prediction is vital for risk-aware emergency planning, yet most deep learning models lack principled uncertainty quantification (UQ). Further, for boundary-sensitive cases like wildfire spread, evaluating models with global metrics alone is often insufficient. To shift the focus of UQ evaluation toward a more operationally relevant approach, the Fire-Centered Evaluation Region (FCER) framework is introduced as a spatially conditioned protocol to characterize UQ within critical fire zones. Using FCER, an Ensemble is compared against an distilled single-pass student model on the WildfireSpreadTS dataset. The student model demonstrates comparable calibration and complementary uncertainty ranking in boundary-relevant regimes. Code is available at https://github. com/jonasvilhofunk/WildfireUQ-FCER

</details>


### [57] [DINO Soars: DINOv3 for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation of Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03175)
*Ryan Faulkenberry,Saurabh Prasad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAFe-DINO模型，利用DINOv3骨干网络实现无需遥感（RS）领域微调的开放词汇语义分割（OVSS），通过成本聚合与无训练特征上采样提升RS影像分割性能，并在多个RS数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感领域缺乏密集标注数据，获取成本高；现有无监督或弱监督分割方法性能落后于有监督方法，亟需不依赖RS领域微调的强泛化分割方案。

Method: 基于DINOv3骨干网络构建CAFe-DINO模型，引入成本聚合（Cost Aggregation）和无训练的文本-图像相似度分数上采样（Feature Upsampling），并在COCO-Stuff的RS子集上进行轻量微调，而非在RS数据上微调。

Result: CAFe-DINO在关键遥感分割数据集上达到SOTA性能，超越所有在RS数据上微调的开放词汇分割方法。

Conclusion: DINOv3强大的跨域表征能力可直接迁移至遥感分割任务，结合轻量适配模块即可实现高性能、免RS微调的开放词汇分割，为资源受限的RS应用提供新范式。

Abstract: The remote sensing (RS) domain suffers from a lack of densely labeled datasets, which are costly to obtain. Thus, models that can segment RS imagery well without supervised fine-tuning are valuable, but existing solutions fall behind supervised methods. Recently, DINOv3 surpassed SOTA RS foundation models on the GEO-bench segmentation benchmark without pre-training on RS data. Additionally, DINO.txt has enabled open vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) with the DINOv3 backbone. We leverage these developments to form an OVSS model for RS imagery, free of RS-domain fine-tuning. Our model, CAFe-DINO (Cost Aggregation + Feature Upsampling with DINO) exploits the strong OVSS performance of DINOv3 for RS imagery via cost aggregation and training-free upsampling of text-image similarity scores. The robust latent of the DINOv3 backbone eliminates the need for fine-tuning on RS imagery; we instead fine-tune our model on a RS-targeted subset of COCO-Stuff. CAFe-DINO achieves state-of-the-art performance on key RS segmentation datasets, outperforming OVSS methods fine-tuned on RS data. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/rfaulk/DINO_Soars.

</details>


### [58] [Sentinel2Cap: A Human-Annotated Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Remote Sensing Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03189)
*Lucrezia Tosato,Gianluca Lombardi,Ronny Hansch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Sentinel2Cap，一个用于多模态卫星图像（特别是SAR和多光谱）的新型人工标注图像描述数据集，并在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型上进行了零样本图像描述实验，验证了不同模态（RGB、多光谱、SAR伪彩色）的表现差异及上下文提示的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像描述数据集主要面向自然图像或高分辨率光学遥感影像，缺乏针对合成孔径雷达（SAR）和中等分辨率多模态卫星数据的高质量人工标注描述数据集。

Method: 构建Sentinel2Cap数据集，包含Sentinel-1 SAR与Sentinel-2多光谱影像（10m/20m分辨率）配对图像块及人工撰写并严格校验的描述文本；采用Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型进行零样本图像描述生成，并对比RGB、多光谱、SAR伪彩色三种模态表现，同时引入模态特定上下文提示进行消融分析。

Result: RGB模态描述性能最优，SAR模态最具挑战性；加入模态特定上下文提示可稳定提升所有评估指标；验证了人工标注多模态遥感描述数据的价值。

Conclusion: Sentinel2Cap填补了多模态卫星图像（尤其是SAR）描述数据集的空白，实验证明其对推动跨模态遥感场景理解研究具有重要意义，所有数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Image captioning has become an important task in computer vision, enabling models to generate natural language descriptions of visual content. While several datasets exist for natural images and high-resolution optical remote sensing imagery, the availability of captioning datasets for multimodal satellite data remains limited, particularly for SAR imagery and medium-resolution sensors. We introduce Sentinel2Cap, a human-annotated multimodal captioning dataset containing Sentinel-1 SAR and Sentinel-2 multi-spectral image patches at 10 m and 20 m spatial resolution with diverse land cover compositions. Captions are created manually and carefully validated to ensure both semantic accuracy and linguistic quality. To evaluate Sentinel2Cap, we perform a zero-shot captioning using the Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct model across three image modalities: RGB, multi-spectral, and SAR pseudo-RGB representations. Results show that RGB images achieve the highest captioning performance, while SAR images remain more challenging for vision-language models. Providing modality-specific contextual prompts consistently improves performance across all metrics. These findings highlight both the challenges of multimodal remote sensing image captioning and the potential value of human-annotated datasets for advancing research in cross-modal scene understanding. All the material is publicly avaiable.

</details>


### [59] [Synthetic Data Generation for Long-Tail Medical Image Classification: A Case Study in Skin Lesions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03221)
*Jiaxiang Jiang,Mahesh Subedar,Omesh Tickoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的合成数据增强方法，用于解决医学图像长尾分类问题，显著提升了尾部稀有病类别的诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾类别分布普遍存在于多类医学数据集中，导致深度学习模型在样本稀少的尾部类别（常对应高风险疾病）上表现不佳，现有方法改进有限且难以扩展。

Method: 提出一种基于扩散模型的合成数据增强流程，包括新颖的修复式扩散模型和分布外（OOD）后筛选机制，以生成多样、真实且具有临床意义的合成样本。

Result: 在ISIC2019皮肤病变数据集上验证，整体性能显著提升，尾部最少样本类别准确率提高超28%。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的数据增强能有效缓解医学长尾不平衡问题，增强分类鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-tailed class distributions are pervasive in multi-class medical datasets and pose significant challenges for deep learning models which typically underperform on tail classes with limited samples. This limitation is particularly problematic in medical applications, where rare classes often correspond to severe or high-risk diseases and therefore require high diagnostic accuracy. Existing solutions-including specialized architectures, rebalanced loss functions, and handcrafted data augmentation-offer only marginal improvements and struggle to scale due to their limited and largely deterministic variability. To address these challenges, we introduce a diffusion-model-driven synthetic data augmentation pipeline tailored for medical long-tailed classification. Our approach features a novel inpainting diffusion model combined with an Out-of-Distribution (OOD) post-selection mechanism to ensure diverse, realistic, and clinically meaningful synthetic samples. Evaluated on the ISIC2019 skin lesion classification dataset, one of the largest and most imbalanced medical imaging benchmarks, our method yields substantial improvements in overall performance, with particularly pronounced gains on tail classes with more than $28\%$ improvement on the class with the fewest samples. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of diffusion-based augmentation in mitigating long-tail imbalance and enhancing medical classification robustness.

</details>


### [60] [CropVLM: A Domain-Adapted Vision-Language Model for Open-Set Crop Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03259)
*Abderrahmene Boudiaf,Sajd Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CropVLM——一种面向农业领域的视觉-语言模型，结合领域特定语义对齐（DSSA）与Hybrid Open-Set Localization Network（HOS-Net），实现无需物种特异性标注的零样本作物检测与分类，显著缓解植物表型分析中的‘表型瓶颈’。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高通量植物表型分析受限于人工耗时、主观偏差及闭集视觉模型依赖大量物种特异性标注、泛化能力差的问题，亟需可跨物种、免重训练的开放集识别方案。

Method: 提出CropVLM视觉-语言模型，通过领域特定语义对齐（DSSA）在52,987组覆盖37个物种的田间图像-文本对上训练；并设计HOS-Net架构，将CropVLM集成用于仅凭自然语言描述即可定位新作物，无需微调。

Result: CropVLM在零样本分类中达72.51%准确率，超越7个CLIP基线；检测 pipeline 在CVTCropDet基准和热带水果数据集上分别取得49.17和50.73 AP50，显著优于次优方法（34.89和48.58）。

Conclusion: CropVLM与HOS-Net构成可扩展、免标注的农业视觉理解框架，有效支撑大规模育种与生物多样性研究，推动可持续农业发展。

Abstract: High-throughput plant phenotyping, the quantitative measurement of observable plant traits, is critical for modern breeding but remains constrained by a "phenotyping bottleneck," where manual data collection is labor-intensive and prone to observer bias. Conventional closed-set computer vision systems fail to address this challenge, as they require extensive species-specific annotation and lack the flexibility to handle diverse breeding populations. To bridge this gap, we present CropVLM, a Vision-Language Model (VLM) adapted for the agricultural domain via Domain-Specific Semantic Alignment (DSSA). Trained on 52,987 manually selected image-caption pairs covering 37 species in natural field conditions, CropVLM effectively maps agronomic terminology to fine-grained visual features. We further introduce the Hybrid Open-Set Localization Network (HOS-Net), an architecture that integrates CropVLM to enable the detection of novel crops solely from natural language descriptions without retraining. By eliminating the reliance on species-specific training data, CropVLM provides a scalable solution for high-throughput phenotyping, accelerating genetic gain and facilitating large-scale biodiversity research essential for sustainable agriculture. The trained model weights and complete pipeline implementation are publicly available at: [https://github.com/boudiafA/CropVLM](https://github.com/boudiafA/CropVLM). In comprehensive evaluations, CropVLM achieves 72.51% zero-shot classification accuracy, outperforming seven CLIP-style baselines. Our detection pipeline demonstrates superior zero-shot generalization to novel species, achieving 49.17 AP50 on our CVTCropDet benchmark and 50.73 AP50 on tropical fruit species, compared to 34.89 and 48.58 for the next-best method, respectively.

</details>


### [61] [VEBench:Benchmarking Large Multimodal Models for Real-World Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03276)
*Andong Deng,Dawei Du,Zhenfang Chen,Wen Zhong,Fan Chen,Guang Chen,Chia-Wen Kuo,Longyin Wen,Chen Chen,Sijie Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向真实视频编辑场景的综合基准VEBENCH，用于评估大模型在编辑知识理解和操作推理两方面的能力，并揭示了当前多模态大模型与人类编辑认知之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实视频编辑需要影视技术专业知识和多模态推理能力，而现有大语言多模态模型（LMMs）在多视频推理和实际编辑工作流方面的评估尚属空白。

Method: 构建了包含3.9K高质量剪辑视频（257小时）和3080个人工验证问答对的VEBENCH基准；采用三轮人机协同标注流程确保时序标注精度和语义一致性；设计两个互补任务：视频编辑技术识别（7种技术）和视频编辑操作模拟（多候选片段选择与时序定位）。

Result: 在Gemini-2.5-Pro等闭源及多个开源LMM上实验表明，当前模型在两项任务上性能远低于人类水平，尤其在操作模拟任务中表现更弱。

Conclusion: VEBENCH填补了视频编辑智能评估的空白，揭示了将视频理解与创造性操作推理结合的迫切需求，为未来智能视频编辑系统研究提供了基础支撑。

Abstract: Real-world video editing demands not only expert knowledge of cinematic techniques but also multimodal reasoning to select, align, and combine footage into coherent narratives. While recent Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have shown remarkable progress in general video understanding, their abilities in multi-video reasoning and operational editing workflows remain largely unexplored. We introduce VEBENCH, the first comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate both editing knowledge understanding and operational reasoning in realistic video editing scenarios. VEBENCH contains 3.9K high-quality edited videos (over 257 hours) and 3,080 human-verified QA pairs, built through a three-round human-AI collaborative annotation pipeline that ensures precise temporal labeling and semantic consistency. It features two complementary QA tasks: 1) Video Editing Technique Recognition, assessing models' ability to identify 7 editing techniques using multimodal cues; and 2) Video Editing Operation Simulation, modeling real-world editing workflows by requiring the selection and temporal localization of relevant clips from multiple candidates. Extensive experiments across proprietary (e.g., Gemini-2.5-Pro) and open-source LMMs reveal a large gap between current model performance and human-level editing cognition. These results highlight the urgent need for bridging video understanding with creative operational reasoning. We envision VEBENCH as a foundation for advancing intelligent video editing systems and driving future research on complex reasoning.

</details>


### [62] [FACTOR: Counterfactual Training-Free Test-Time Adaptation for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03294)
*Kaixiang Zhao,Mao Ye,Lihua Zhou,Hu Wang,Luping Ji,Song Tang,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FACTOR框架，通过反事实推理在开放词汇目标检测中实现无需训练的测试时自适应，以提升模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇目标检测在分布偏移下易受非因果视觉属性（如亮度、纹理）的伪相关干扰，现有测试时自适应方法或依赖昂贵在线优化，或忽略属性特异性问题。

Method: FACTOR通过沿非因果属性扰动测试图像，比较原始与反事实视图的区域级预测，量化属性敏感性、语义相关性和预测变化，从而选择性抑制依赖属性的预测，且无需参数更新。

Result: 在PASCAL-C、COCO-C和FoggyCityscapes上的实验表明，FACTOR持续优于现有TTA方法。

Conclusion: 显式的反事实推理能有效提升开放词汇目标检测在分布偏移下的鲁棒性，且无需训练或参数更新。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection often fails under distribution shifts, as it can be misled by spurious correlations between non-causal visual attributes (e.g., brightness, texture) and object categories. Existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods either depend on costly online optimization or perform global calibration, overlooking the attribute-specific nature of these failures. To address this, we propose FACTOR (counterFACtual training-free Test-time adaptation for Open-vocabulaRy object detection), a lightweight framework grounded in counterfactual reasoning. By perturbing test images along non-causal attributes and comparing region-level predictions between original and counterfactual views, FACTOR quantifies attribute sensitivity, semantic relevance, and prediction variation to selectively suppress attribute-dependent predictions-without parameter updates. Experiments on PASCAL-C, COCO-C, and FoggyCityscapes show that FACTOR consistently outperforms prior TTA methods, demonstrating that explicit counterfactual reasoning effectively improves robustness under distribution shifts.

</details>


### [63] [TACO: Trajectory Aligning Cross-view Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03315)
*Tavis Shore,Oscar Mendez,Simon Hadfield*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TACO，一种紧耦合IMU与细粒度跨视角地理定位（CVGL）的实时定位系统，仅需初始一次GNSS读数，之后完全依赖机载传感器，在KITTI数据集上将中位绝对轨迹误差（ATE）从97.0m显著降至16.3m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细粒度CVGL方法仅作为单次定位器评估，未在真实导航流水线中验证；而IMU存在无界漂移问题，亟需可靠、低开销的绝对定位锚点以实现长期自主定位。

Method: 提出TACO流水线：基于解析的横轨误差模型动态触发CVGL；采用前向偏置的五点多重裁剪搜索控制推理成本；引入偏航残差门控剔除与罗盘不一致的定位结果；设计各向异性体坐标系噪声模型，依据每次CVGL结果置信度自适应调整UKF更新；结合经筛选的闭环因子图实现离线轨迹优化。

Result: 在KITTI raw数据集上，TACO将中位ATE从IMU-only的97.0m降至16.3m（提升5.9倍），每帧融合耗时<0.1ms，相机占空比仅5–10%。

Conclusion: TACO成功实现了IMU与细粒度CVGL的高效紧耦合，显著提升了长时序下的绝对定位精度与鲁棒性，为GNSS拒止环境下的实时自主导航提供了实用可行方案。

Abstract: Cross-View Geo-localisation (CVGL) matches ground imagery against satellite tiles to give absolute position fixes, an alternative to GNSS where signals are occluded, jammed, or spoofed. Recent fine-grained CVGL methods regress sub-tile metric pose, but have only been evaluated as one-shot localisers, never as the primary fix in a live pipeline. Inertial sensing provides high-rate relative motion, but accumulates unbounded drift without an absolute anchor. We propose TACO, a tightly-coupled IMU + fine-grained CVGL pipeline that consumes a single GNSS reading at start-up and thereafter operates on onboard sensing alone. A closed-form cross-track error model triggers CVGL before IMU drift exceeds the matcher's capture radius, and a forward-biased five-point multi-crop search keeps inference cost fixed at five forward passes per fix. A yaw-residual gate rejects fixes that disagree with the onboard compass, and an anisotropic body-frame noise model scales each Unscented Kalman Filter update by per-fix confidence. A factor graph with vetted loop closures provides an offline smoothed trajectory. On the KITTI raw dataset, TACO reduces median Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) from 97.0m (IMU-only) to 16.3m, a 5.9 times reduction, at <0.1 ms per-frame fusion cost and a 5-10% camera duty cycle. Code is available: github.com/tavisshore/TACO.

</details>


### [64] [AHPA: Adaptive Hierarchical Prior Alignment for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03317)
*Ruibin Min,Yexin Liu,Aimin Pan,Changsheng Lu,Jiafei Wu,Kelu Yao,Xiaogang Xu,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应分层先验对齐（AHPA）方法，通过利用冻结VAE编码器中的多级特征，并结合时间步条件的动态路由机制，在扩散Transformer训练中实现随去噪过程动态调整的表示对齐，从而提升收敛速度与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示对齐方法在整条去噪轨迹中采用固定监督目标或固定对齐粒度，忽视了信噪比变化导致的最优对齐粒度动态变化（高噪声需语义/布局级粗粒度引导，低噪声需空间细节精粒度细化），造成表征失配。

Method: 提出AHPA框架：利用冻结VAE编码器天然的多层级隐空间特征（涵盖局部几何、空间拓扑到粗粒度语义布局），设计时间步条件的动态路由器，沿去噪轨迹自适应选择并加权各层级先验进行对齐。

Result: 实验表明AHPA在多个基准上优于基线方法，加速模型收敛、提升生成质量；不增加推理开销，且无需外部视觉编码器监督。

Conclusion: 动态匹配去噪进程中信噪比变化的分层对齐策略优于静态单层对齐，AHPA为扩散Transformer训练提供了更高效、轻量且自包含的表示对齐范式。

Abstract: Representation alignment has recently emerged as an effective paradigm for accelerating Diffusion Transformer training. Despite their success, existing alignment methods typically impose a fixed supervision target or a fixed alignment granularity throughout the entire denoising trajectory, whether the guidance is provided by external vision encoders, internal self-representations, or VAE-derived features. We argue that such timestep-agnostic alignment is suboptimal because the useful granularity of representation supervision changes systematically with the signal-to-noise ratio. In high-noise regimes, diffusion models benefit more from coarse semantic and layout-level anchoring, whereas in low-noise regimes, the training signal should emphasize spatially detailed and structurally faithful refinement. This non-stationary alignment behavior creates a representational mismatch for static single-level supervisors. To address this issue, we propose Adaptive Hierarchical Prior Alignment (AHPA), a lightweight alignment framework that exploits the hierarchical representations naturally embedded in the frozen VAE encoder. Instead of using only a single compressed latent as the alignment target, AHPA extracts multi-level VAE features that provide complementary priors ranging from local geometry and spatial topology to coarse semantic layout. A timestep-conditioned Dynamic Router adaptively selects and weights these hierarchical priors along the denoising trajectory, thereby synchronizing the alignment granularity with the model's evolving training needs. Extensive experiments show that AHPA improves convergence and generation quality over baselines and incurs no additional inference cost while avoiding external encoder supervision during training.

</details>


### [65] [FreeTimeGS++: Secrets of Dynamic Gaussian Splatting and Their Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03337)
*Lucas Yunkyu Lee,Soonho Kim,Youngwook Kim,Sangmin Kim,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对4D高斯点绘（4DGS）进行了系统性分析，揭示了其性能提升的关键隐含因素，并提出了改进方法FreeTimeGS++，提升了动态表征的稳定性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管4D高斯点绘在动态场景重建中表现优异，但其性能提升的具体驱动因素尚不明确，缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 构建可控基线FreeTimeGS_ours，形式化并复现FreeTimeGS的启发式方法；沿4DGS基本维度进行解构分析；提出FreeTimeGS++，引入门控边缘化和神经速度场。

Result: 发现了高斯持续时间驱动的时间分区现象及光度保真度与时空一致性之间的差异；FreeTimeGS++实现了更优的稳定性和鲁棒动态表征，降低了运行方差。

Conclusion: 本文通过深入剖析4DGS的隐含机制，为该领域提供了可复现、可解释的理论基础与实践框架，并开源代码以推动后续研究。

Abstract: The recent surge in 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) has achieved impressive dynamic scene reconstruction. While these methods demonstrate remarkable performance, the specific drivers behind such gains remain less explored, making a systematic understanding of the underlying principles challenging. In this paper, we perform a comprehensive analysis of these hidden factors to provide a clearer perspective on the 4DGS framework. We first establish a controlled baseline, FreeTimeGS_ours, by formalizing and reproducing the heuristics of the state-of-the-art FreeTimeGS. Using this framework, we dissect 4DGS along its fundamental axes and uncover key secrets, including the emergent temporal partitioning driven by Gaussian durations and the discrepancy between photometric fidelity and spatiotemporal consistency. Based on these insights, we propose FreeTimeGS++, a principled method that employs gated marginalization and neural velocity fields to achieve superior stability and robust dynamic representations. Our approach yields reproducible results with reduced run-to-run variance. We will release our implementation to provide a reliable foundation for future 4DGS research.

</details>


### [66] [MedSR-Vision: Deep Learning Framework for Multi-Domain Medical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03343)
*Subhash Gurappa,Trivikram Satharasi,Yashas Hariprasad,Sundararaj Sitharama Iyengar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MedSR-Vision框架，统一评估MRI、X光、超声、CT和眼底等五种医学影像的超分辨率模型（SRCNN、SwinIR、Real-ESRGAN），在×2/×3/×4倍率下对比保真度、感知质量与结构保持能力，并给出临床选型建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像超分辨率方法在解剖准确性、感知质量及跨模态泛化能力方面仍存在挑战，缺乏统一、多模态、多尺度的评估基准。

Method: 构建MedSR-Vision统一评估框架，涵盖5种医学影像模态和3种放大倍率；对SRCNN、SwinIR和Real-ESRGAN三个代表性模型，采用多维度定量指标（保真度、感知真实感、锐度）进行系统评测。

Result: Real-ESRGAN在高倍率下感知质量与边缘恢复最优；SwinIR在结构与诊断特征保持上表现最佳；SRCNN在低倍率下效率与稳定性更优。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了不同模型在不同模态与尺度下的性能权衡，为临床部署提供选型指南，并建立了标准化评估范式，推动医学超分辨率研究发展。

Abstract: Medical image super-resolution (MedSR) is essential for improving diagnostic precision across diverse imaging modalities such as MRI, CT, X-ray, Ultrasound, and Fundus imaging. Despite rapid advances in deep learning, challenges remain in preserving anatomical accuracy, maintaining perceptual quality, and generalizing across medical domains. This paper presents MedSR-Vision, a novel unified deep learning framework for evaluating and comparing super-resolution models across five modalities: Brain MRI, Chest X-ray, Renal Ultrasound, Nephrolithiasis CT, and Spine MRI, at magnification scales of $\times2$, $\times3$, and $\times4$. Three representative models namely SRCNN, SwinIR, and Real-ESRGAN are benchmarked using multiple quantitative metrics encompassing fidelity, perceptual realism, and sharpness.
  Experimental analysis demonstrates that Real-ESRGAN achieves superior perceptual quality and edge recovery at higher scales, SwinIR excels in preserving structural and diagnostic features, and SRCNN provides efficient and stable performance at lower magnifications. The results establish domain-specific insights and practical guidelines for model selection in clinical imaging workflows, offering a standardized evaluation framework for future medical image super-resolution research and deployment.

</details>


### [67] [VLMaxxing through FrameMogging Training-Free Anti-Recomputation for Video Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03351)
*JF Bastien,Sam D'Amico*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的反重计算（anti-recomputation）策略，通过在视频VLM中复用已验证稳定的视觉状态（如视频特征缓存），显著降低后续查询延迟；实验表明在Qwen2.5-VL模型上实现14.90–35.92倍的跟查加速，同时保持准确性；并引入C-VISION和C-CEILING等机制控制首帧加速与端到端收益的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频VLM重复处理稳定视觉内容（如静止场景），造成计算浪费；需避免对未变化状态重新编码，提升推理效率。

Method: 提出训练无关的自适应状态复用机制：基于验证判断视觉状态是否稳定，若稳定则复用缓存特征；否则触发新视觉编码；引入C-VISION（跳过首帧视觉塔计算）和C-CEILING（按组件耗时占比折算端到端加速）进行精细化控制。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct-4bit上，同视频多轮问答延迟降低14.90–35.92倍，准确率无损；C-VISION在Gemma 4-E4B-4bit上实现1.316x首问加速且无解析失败；压力测试验证50轮重复提问稳定性。

Conclusion: 反重计算是提升视频VLM推理效率的有效范式，未来应构建VLM原生媒体表示，直接暴露运动、状态、时间等语义，减少对逐帧RGB重建的依赖。

Abstract: Video vision-language models (VLMs) keep paying for visual state the stream already told us was stable. The factory wall did not move, but most VLM pipelines still hand the model dense RGB frames or a fresh prefix again. We study that waste as training-free anti-recomputation: reuse state when validation says it survives, and buy fresh evidence when the scene, query, or cache topology requires it.
  The largest measured win is after ingest. On frozen Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct-4bit, adaptive same-video follow-up reuse preserves paired choices and correctness on a 93-query VideoMME breadth setting while reducing follow-up latency by 14.90-35.92x. The first query is still cold; the win starts when later questions reuse the same video state. Stress tests bound the result: repeated-question schedules hold through 50 turns, while dense-answer-anchored prompt variation separates conservative fixed K=1 repair from faster aggressive policies that drift.
  Fresh-video pruning is smaller but real. C-VISION skips timed vision-tower work before the first answer is generated. On Gemma 4-E4B-4bit, the clean 32f short cell reaches 1.316x first-query speedup with no paired drift or parse failures on 20 items; Qwen shows the fidelity/speed boundary.
  Stage-share ceiling (C-CEILING) is the accounting guardrail: a component speedup becomes an end-to-end speedup only in proportion to the wall-clock share it accelerates, so C-VISION and after-ingest follow-up reuse do not multiply. Candidate C-STREAM remains a native-rate target, not a headline result here. The broader direction is VLM-native media that expose change, motion, uncertainty, object state, sensor time, and active tiles directly, so models do not have to rediscover the world from dense RGB every frame.

</details>


### [68] [Can Multimodal Large Language Models Understand Pathologic Movements? A Pilot Study on Seizure Semiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03352)
*Lina Zhang,Tonmoy Monsoor,Mehmet Efe Lorasdagi,Prateik Sinha,Chong Han,Peizheng Li,Yuan Wang,Jessica Pasqua,Colin McCrimmon,Rajarshi Mazumder,Vwani Roychowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究探索了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在癫痫发作视频中自动识别病理运动的潜力，发现其零样本性能优于微调的CNN和ViT模型，尤其擅长识别显著的姿态和上下文特征；通过针对性信号增强（如面部裁剪、姿态估计、音频降噪）可进一步提升性能，并且生成解释具有较高临床可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在日常活动识别中表现优异，但在神经系统疾病中具有临床意义的病理性不自主运动分析方面尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在90段临床癫痫发作视频上，评估了前沿MLLMs对20个ILAE定义的半学术特征的零样本识别能力；对比了微调CNN和ViT基线模型；采用面部裁剪、姿态估计、音频降噪等特征导向信号增强策略；并通过专家评估验证解释的可信度（faithfulness）。

Result: MLLMs在18个特征中胜过基线模型13个；信号增强提升了20个特征中10个的性能；94.3%的正确预测案例所生成解释获得≥60%的专家可信度评分。

Conclusion: 通用MLLMs经针对性预处理策略适配后，可在临床视频分析中实现高效、可解释的辅助诊断，展现出巨大应用潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated robust capabilities in recognizing everyday human activities, yet their potential for analyzing clinically significant involuntary movements in neurological disorders remains largely unexplored. This pilot study evaluates the capability of MLLMs for automated recognition of pathological movements in seizure videos. We assessed the zero-shot performance of state-of-the-art MLLMs on 20 ILAE-defined semiological features across 90 clinical seizure recordings. MLLMs outperformed fine-tuned Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) and Vision Transformer (ViT) baseline models on 13 of 18 features without task-specific training, demonstrating particular strength in recognizing salient postural and contextual features while struggling with subtle, high-frequency movements. Feature-targeted signal enhancement (facial cropping, pose estimation, audio denoising) improved performance on 10 of 20 features. Expert evaluation showed that 94.3 percent of MLLM-generated explanations for correctly predicted cases achieved at least 60 percent faithfulness scores, aligning with epileptologist reasoning. These findings demonstrate the potential of adapting general-purpose MLLMs for specialized clinical video analysis through targeted preprocessing strategies, offering a path toward interpretable, efficient diagnostic assistance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LinaZhangUCLA/PathMotionMLLM.

</details>


### [69] [Tracing Like a Clinician: Anatomy-Guided Spatial Priors for Cephalometric Landmark Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03358)
*Sidhartha Mohapatra,Pallavi Mohanty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解剖学引导的五阶段初始化流程，将正畸医生的手工头影测量描迹工作流转化为计算操作，生成空间注意力先验以提升下游关键点检测器性能，在多设备数据集上显著提升了定位精度并验证了解剖先验对泛化能力的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化系统未能模拟正畸医生在头影测量描迹中遵循的结构化临床推理流程（如软组织轮廓识别、颅骨区域划分、轮廓描迹与几何定义下的标志点定位），导致泛化能力不足。

Method: 设计五阶段解剖学引导初始化管道，将临床工作流转化为计算操作，生成置信度加权的空间注意力先验，用于增强HRNet-W32关键点检测器；并在来自三个数据源、涵盖7种以上成像设备的1502张X光片上进行评估；通过三向消融实验分析解剖先验的作用。

Result: 在25个标志点上达到1.04 mm平均径向误差，较先前SOTA（19点，1.23 mm）提升15.4%，其中12个点误差低于1 mm；消融实验证明移除解剖先验会导致测试集误差从1.04 mm激增至1.94 mm，而随机高斯先验更差（2.24 mm）。

Conclusion: 将临床领域知识编码为空间先验可提供架构和数据增强无法替代的归纳偏置，显著提升模型泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Abstract: When orthodontists trace cephalometric radiographs, they follow a structured workflow: identify the soft tissue profile, partition the skull into anatomical regions, trace contours, and locate landmarks using geometric definitions -- yet no automated system replicates this reasoning. We present a five-phase anatomy-guided initialization pipeline that translates this clinical workflow into computational operations, producing confidence-weighted spatial attention priors for a downstream HRNet-W32 detector. On 1,502 radiographs from three sources spanning 7+ imaging devices, the system achieves 1.04 mm mean radial error on 25 landmarks -- surpassing prior state-of-the-art (1.23 mm on 19 landmarks) by 15.4%, with twelve landmarks below 1 mm. A three-way controlled ablation reveals two striking findings. First, removing anatomical priors does not merely slow convergence -- it destroys generalization: both models converge to ~1.03 mm on validation, but diverge to 1.94 vs. 1.04 mm on the test set. Second, replacing anatomical priors with random-position Gaussians produces even worse generalization (2.24 mm), confirming that the improvement derives from anatomically correct positioning, not additional input channels. Clinical domain knowledge encoded as spatial priors provides an inductive bias that architecture and data augmentation alone do not provide.

</details>


### [70] [Mix3R: Mixing Feed-forward Reconstruction and Generative 3D Priors for Joint Multi-view Aligned 3D Reconstruction and Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03359)
*Siyou Lin,Zhou Xue,Hongwen Zhang,Liang An,Dongping Li,Shaohui Jiao,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mix3R是一种新型的生成式3D重建方法，通过将前馈重建与3D生成融合为一个对齐框架，在稀疏视角下实现高精度、输入对齐的3D形状重建和相机姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏视角3D重建方法存在两极分化：前馈重建缺乏完整几何结构但像素对齐好；生成式重建能生成完整几何但输入对齐差。本文旨在统一二者优势，实现既完整又对齐的重建。

Method: Mix3R采用两阶段生成：1）稀疏体素生成阶段，利用混合Transformer架构联合输出稀疏体素、每视角点图及对齐相机参数；2）纹理化几何生成阶段，基于重叠注意力偏置，无需训练即可将输入纹理精准映射到生成形状上。

Result: 在稀疏视角重建任务中，Mix3R生成的3D形状比纯生成方法更贴合输入图像，同时相机姿态估计精度优于前馈重建方法。

Conclusion: Mix3R成功桥接了前馈重建与生成式3D重建，通过双向条件建模实现了几何完整性与输入对齐性的协同提升，为稀疏视角重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent trends in sparse-view 3D reconstruction have taken two different paths: feed-forward reconstruction that predicts pixel-aligned point maps without a complete geometry, and generative 3D reconstruction that generates complete geometry but often with poor input-alignment. We present Mix3R, a novel generative 3D reconstruction method which mixes feed-forward reconstruction and 3D generation into a single framework in an aligned manner. Mix3R generates a 3D shape in two stages: a sparse voxel generation stage and a textured geometry generation stage. Unlike pure generative methods, our first-stage generation jointly produces a coarse 3D structure (sparse voxels), per-view point maps and camera parameters aligned to that 3D structure. This is made possible by introducing a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that inserts global self-attentions to a feed-forward reconstruction model and a 3D generative model, both pretrained on large-scale data. This design effectively retains the pretrained priors but enables better 2D-3D alignment. Based on the initial aligned generations of sparse 3D voxels and point maps, we compute an overlap-based attention bias that is directly added to another pretrained textured geometry generation model, enabling it to correctly place input textures onto generated shapes in a training-free manner. Our design brings mutual benefits to both feed-forward reconstruction and 3D generation: The feed-forward branch learns to ground its predictions to a generative 3D prior, and conversely, the 3D generation branch is conditioned on geometrically informative features from the feed-forward branch. As a result, our method produces 3D shapes with better input alignment compared with pure 3D generative methods, together with camera pose estimations more accurate than previous feed-forward reconstruction methods. Our project page is at https://jsnln.github.io/mix3r/

</details>


### [71] [Dynamic Distillation and Gradient Consistency for Robust Long-Tailed Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03364)
*Taigo Sakai,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对长尾类增量学习（LT-CIL）的新方法，通过梯度一致性正则化和基于归一化熵的蒸馏损失自适应加权，缓解灾难性遗忘、 minority class under-learning 和 majority class overfitting 问题，在多个长尾基准上提升最高达5.0%准确率，尤其在‘In-ordered’设定下表现突出，且计算开销低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LT-CIL场景中，类别长尾分布加剧了持续学习固有的灾难性遗忘问题，同时导致少数类学习不足和多数类过拟合。

Method: 提出两种技术：1）梯度一致性正则化，利用梯度滑动平均抑制训练波动；2）基于归一化熵动态调整蒸馏损失权重，以平衡旧知识保留与新知识获取。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-LT、ImageNetSubset-LT和Food101-LT上准确率最高提升5.0%，在‘In-ordered’设定下效果尤为显著，且计算开销无明显增加。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效缓解LT-CIL中的多重挑战，兼具性能提升与实用性。

Abstract: The task of Long-tailed Class Incremental Learning (LT-CIL) addresses the sequential learning of new classes from datasets with imbalanced class distributions. This scenario intensifies the fundamental problem of catastrophic forgetting, inherent to continual learning, with the dual challenges of under-learning minority classes and overfitting majority classes. To tackle these combined issues, this paper proposes two main techniques. First, we introduce gradient consistency regularization, which leverages the moving average of gradients to suppress abrupt fluctuations and stabilize the training process. Second, we dynamically adjust the weight of the distillation loss by measuring the degree of class imbalance with normalized entropy. This adaptive weighting establishes an optimal balance between retaining old knowledge and acquiring new information. Experiments on the CIFAR-100-LT, ImageNetSubset-LT, and Food101-LT benchmarks show that our method achieves consistent accuracy improvements of up to 5.0\%. Furthermore, we demonstrate dramatic gains in the challenging 'In-ordered' setting, where tasks progress from majority to minority classes, highlighting our method's robustness in mitigating forgetting under unfavorable learning dynamics. This enhanced performance is achieved without a significant increase in computational overhead, demonstrating the practicality of our framework.

</details>


### [72] [Dual-Foundation Models for Unsupervised Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03365)
*Yerin Cheon,Aruna Balasubramanian,Francois Rameau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双基础模型（SAM和DINOv3）的无监督域自适应（UDA）框架，用于语义分割，以克服传统方法依赖高置信伪标签和原型不稳定的问题，在GTA-to-Cityscapes和SYNTHIA-to-Cityscapes上分别提升1.3%和1.4% mIoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无监督域自适应中因合成与真实数据间域差距导致的性能瓶颈，尤其是现有方法受限于高置信伪标签覆盖范围窄及原型初始化偏差大、不稳定的问题。

Method: 提出双基础模型驱动的UDA框架：1）利用SAM结合超像素引导提示，拓展目标域低置信区域的学习；2）引入DINOv3构建鲁棒、域不变的类原型，替代源域预训练原型。

Result: 在GTA-to-Cityscapes和SYNTHIA-to-Cityscapes两个标准UDA基准上，mIoU分别较强基线提升+1.3%和+1.4%。

Conclusion: 融合视觉基础模型（SAM与DINOv3）可有效缓解UDA中伪标签覆盖不足与原型偏差问题，为语义分割的跨域迁移提供更鲁棒、泛化性更强的新范式。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation provides pixel-level scene understanding essential for autonomous driving and fine-grained perception tasks. However, training segmentation models requires costly, labor-intensive annotations on real-world datasets. Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA) addresses this by training models on labeled synthetic data and adapting them to unlabeled real images. While conceptually simple, adaptation is challenging due to the domain gap, i.e., differences in visual appearance and scene structure between synthetic and real data. Prior approaches bridge this gap through pixel-level mixing or feature-level contrastive learning. Yet, these techniques suffer from two major limitations: (1) reliance on high-confidence pseudo-labels restricts learning to a subset of the target domain, and (2) prototype-based contrastive methods initialize class prototypes from source-trained models, yielding biased and unstable anchors during adaptation. To address these issues, we propose a dual-foundation UDA framework that leverages two complementary foundation models. First, we employ the Segment Anything Model (SAM) with superpixel-guided prompting to enable learning from a broader range of target pixels beyond high-confidence predictions. Second, we incorporate DINOv3 to construct stable, domain-invariant class prototypes through its robust representation learning. Our method achieves consistent improvements of +1.3% and +1.4% mIoU over strong UDA baselines on GTA-to-Cityscapes and SYNTHIA-to-Cityscapes, respectively.

</details>


### [73] [SoDa2: Single-Stage Open-Set Domain Adaptation via Decoupled Alignment for Cross-Scene Hyperspectral Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03371)
*Yiwen Liu,Minghua Wang,Jing Yao,Xin Zhao,Gemine Vivone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单阶段开集域自适应方法SoDa²，通过解耦光谱与空间特征对齐、贡献感知双模态特征提取及高斯混合模型建模相似性分布，有效解决跨场景高光谱图像分类中的域偏移与计算成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开集跨场景高光谱图像分类方法面临两大挑战：混合光谱-空间特征直接对齐导致的域偏移，以及两阶段训练策略带来的高计算成本。

Method: 提出单阶段开集域自适应方法SoDa²，包含贡献感知双模态特征提取、解耦对齐模块（分别最小化光谱和空间差异的最大均值差异MMD）以及单阶段双分支框架；利用高斯混合模型建模两类特征间余弦相似度平方分布以实现无先验未知类识别。

Result: 在三组高光谱数据集上的大量实验表明，SoDa²在分类精度和模型迁移能力上均优于当前最优方法。

Conclusion: SoDa²通过解耦对齐与单阶段设计，在保证性能的同时显著降低计算开销，为开集跨场景高光谱图像分类提供了高效鲁棒的新范式。

Abstract: Cross-scene hyperspectral image (HSI) classification stands as a fundamental research topic in remote sensing, with extensive applications spanning various fields. Owing to the inclusion of unknown categories in the target domain and the existence of domain shift across different scenes, open-set domain adaptation techniques are commonly employed to address cross-scene HSI classification. However, existing open-set cross-scene HSI classification methods still face two critical challenges: (1) domain shift issues arising from the direct alignment of mixed spectral-spatial features; (2) high computational costs caused by two-stage training strategies. To address these issues, this paper proposes a single-stage open-set domain adaptation method with decoupled alignment (SoDa$^2$) for cross-scene HSI classification. A contribution-aware dual-modality feature extraction is customized to disentangle the characteristics from spectral sequence signals and spatial details, selectively and adaptively enhancing discriminative features. The decoupled alignment module minimizes the Maximum Mean Discrepancy to independently reduce the spectral discrepancy and the spatial discrepancy between the source and target domains, extracting more fine-grained domain-invariant features. A cost-effective single-stage dual-branch framework is designed to learn MMD-constrainted aligned features and constraint-free intrinsic features for adaptive distinction between known and unknown classes. This framework employs a Gaussian Mixture Model to model the squared cosine similarity distribution between the two feature types, enabling open-set recognition without prior knowledge of unknown classes. Extensive experiments on three groups of HSI datasets demonstrate that SoDa$^2$ outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior classification accuracy and model transferability for open-set cross-scene tasks.

</details>


### [74] [Enhancing Self-Supervised Talking Head Forgery Detection via a Training-Free Dual-System Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03390)
*Ke Liu,Jiwei Wei,Shuchang Zhou,Yutong Xiao,Ruikun Chai,Yitong Qin,Yuyang Zhou,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的双系统（TFDS）框架，用于提升现有基于分数的自监督说话头伪造检测器在难例上的判别能力，通过轻量级阈值路由（System-1）与证据引导的细粒度重排序（System-2）协同优化异常分数排序，显著增强跨生成器泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分数的自监督说话头伪造检测器对难例判别能力不足，表现为异常分数排序不可靠，其潜在判别线索尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: 提出训练免费的双系统（TFDS）框架：System-1采用轻量阈值路由将样本分为高置信与不确定子集；System-2仅对不确定子集进行证据引导的细粒度重排序，修正原始分数分布中的相对顺序。
